Flip delta functions:
We need index mapping for full correlate length L, lag l from -(n-1) to n-1, index = n-1 + l.
For position p, define flip_delta(p, delta_h) where delta_h=+1 for 0->1, -1 for 1->0. Then Δc_l = delta_h * (d[p-l] - h[p+l])? Let's derive: If h_p changes by δ, d_p changes by -δ. c_l = d_n h_{n+l}. Terms:
- h at p: n=p-l → d_{p-l} δ if valid
- d at p: n=p → h_{p+l} (-δ) if valid
So Δc_l = δ * (d[p-l] - h[p+l]). Yes. For 0->1 δ=+1, 1->0 δ=-1 matches.
Cross for swap p (δ=+1 if zero) and q (δ=-1 if one): The simultaneous change has extra Δd_p Δh_q? General c = sum d_n h_{n+l}. For each term, Δ = d_n Δh_{n+l} + h_{n+l} Δd_n + Δd_n Δh_{n+l}. Summing linear terms as above for p and q includes all terms where one endpoint changed, but for terms with both endpoints (n=p,n+l=q and n=q,n+l=p), the linear terms include d_p Δh_q etc. Need add Δd_p Δh_q for both orientations. Δd_p=-δ_p, Δh_q=δ_q. For swap δ_p=+1, δ_q=-1 => Δd_p=-1, Δh_q=-1 => +1. Other orientation: Δd_q=+1, Δh_p=+1=>+1. Thus extra +1 at l = q-p and p-q for any swap (if p≠q). Exactly. Good.

So candidate new c = c + δ_p V_p + δ_q V_q + cross, where V_p(l)=d[p-l]-h[p+l] (as array). δ_p * V_p. For zero p, δ=+1; for one q, δ=-1. This formula can compute candidate max via numpy if we can produce V_p + (-V_q) quickly.

Could recompute per position? Or maintain base? V_p depends on h,d at positions near p. To update c for an accepted swap, we need c += V_i - V_j + cross.  def apply_flip(c_arr, p, sign): # sign +1 for 0->1, -1 for 1->0 maybe
   if sign==1:

def binary_descent(h, n, deadline, target=None):
  m=n//2, h int8, d=1-h int8, c=corr_int.
  M=c.max()
  best_M=M; best_h=h.copy()
  ones = np.flatnonzero(h); zeros = np.flatnonzero(~h)
  rng.
  while time < deadline:
    lags = indices where c == M (or maybe > best_M?)
    candidates = set()
    # choose top K lags (if many)
    # For each lag l, contributors:
    if l >=0: start=0, length=n-l; pos = start + np.where((h[:length]==0)&(h[l:]==1))[0]
       for each a=pos: i=a (zero), j=a+l (one)
    else: d=-l; n range d..n-1; term h[n-d] (one), d[n] (zero). Positions of zero high: np.where((h[d:]==0) & (h[:-d]==1))[0] + d (zero index i=n), j=n-d (one). Or unify using lag: a from max(0|-l) to n-1-|l|; condition h[a]==0 and h[a+l]==1; but if l negative, a+n positive. Since h[a+l] valid. Use a_start = max(0, -l), a_end = min(n, n+l) because a+l < n => a < n-l. For l positive a in [0,n-l); for l negative a in [-l,n). Condition h[a]==0 & h[a+l]==1. Then candidate i=a (zero), j=a+l (one). This works for both signs! Check l negative: a high? If l=-d, a from d to n, h[a]=0 high, h[a-d]=1 low. i=a zero high, j=a-d one low. Flip i 0->1 and j 1->0, correct.
    Evaluate candidates; choose one with minimal newM (or accept first below M).
    Could generate candidates from all top lags. To avoid duplicates, use set of (min(i,j), max(i,j))? Since move determined by unordered but oriented i zero j one; set of (i,j).
    If no improvement, try random moves / annealing.

  # add V_i
  temp[p_i] += d_rev? But d_rev changes? We have d_rev = d[::-1] as int8 array.

  temp[p_j] -= d_rev
  temp[n-1-j:2n-2-j] += h[:n-j]
  # cross
  temp[n-1 + (j-i)] +=1
  temp[n-1 + (i-j)] +=1
  return temp.max()

Function `evaluate_candidate(h, c, i, j, n, d, d_rev)`:
  temp = c.copy()
  p = n
  # i is zero
  temp[i:i+n] += d_rev
  temp[n-1-i:2*n-2-i] -= h[:n-i]
  # j is one
  temp[j:j+n] -= d_rev
  temp[n-1-j:2*n-2-j] += h[:n-j]
  k1 = n-1 + j - i
  k2 = n-1 + i - j
  temp[k1] += 1
  temp[k2] += 1
  return int(temp.max()), temp

Candidate generation:
```
def candidate_moves_from_lag(c, h, l, max_candidates):
    if l >= 0:
       a = np.arange(n - l)
    else:
       a = np.arange(-l, n)
    mask = (h[a] == 0) & (h[a+l] == 1)
    positions = a[mask]
    moves = [(a, a+l) for a in positions]
```

Annealing details:
- State M integer.
- Use schedule T from 2 to 0.01 over iterations.
- Candidate generation:
   * From top non-improving lags: if no move improves, still enumerate random contributors from lags with c >= M-2.
   * Random: sample e.g. 20 random pairs.
- Choose candidate:
   - if finding best improvement among limited candidates, accept best if newM < M.
   - else choose a random candidate from pool and accept via Metropolis: if newM < M accept, if == M accept with probability p (maybe 0.3), if >M accept with prob exp(-(newM-M)/T) (newM-M likely 1 or 2).
- Track best and best_h when M < best_M (or exact C? binary C=M*2/n).
- If best_M reaches target_count for current n, maybe continue to see lower.

But simulated annealing with many random candidates may be slow. We can combine: deterministic best-improvement over candidates for descent; after stagnation, perturb by selecting k random moves accepted if not worse than M+1, then descent.

Maybe implement "walk" to escape local minima:
- Repeat: find best improving candidate among top contributors + random. If found, accept; increments no_improve=0.

Evaluation of a candidate:
```
def eval_swap(c, h, i, j, n, d_rev, tmp=None):
   if i == j: return current? no
   tmp = c.copy()
   tmp[i:i+n] += d_rev
   tmp[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h[:n-i]
   tmp[j:j+n] -= d_rev
   tmp[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] += h[:n-j]
   k1 = n-1+j-i; k2 = n-1+i-j
   tmp[k1] += 1; tmp[k2] += 1
   return int(tmp.max()), tmp
```

Candidate selection algorithm for each iteration:
```
def next_move(h,c,n,M, work, rng, mode):

Let's design binary search as a function with phases:
1. Deterministic steep descent: for up to X seconds, repeatedly generate candidates from current M lags and random; choose best newM<M; if none, break.
2. Plateau/anneal: for up to Y seconds, use a dynamic candidate pool; accept moves if newM<M always, if newM==M with prob 0.5 (or if random), if newM>M with prob anneal. Track best. This can escape.
3. Repeat deterministic.

But code complexity. A simpler robust method: Run many independent "greedy descent with random restarts". Each restart: create initial (parent/random), perform greedy until no improvement, record. Many restarts maybe enough. The parent local min maybe accessible.

Pseudo:
```
def binary_improve(h, n, deadline, seed, max_states=..., target_c5=None):
    h = h.astype(np.int8).copy()
    # ensure m ones: if not, threshold
    if h.sum()!=n//2: h=make_binary_sum(h,n)
    d = 1-h; d_rev = d[::-1]
    c = np.correlate(h.astype(np.int32), d.astype(np.int32), 'full').astype(np.int32)
    M = int(c.max())
    best_M = M; best_h = h.copy()
    best_c5 = M*2.0/n
    work = np.empty_like(c)
    tabu = set()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    t_last = time.time()
    stagnation = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
       # update hot indices
       hot_idx = np.nonzero(c >= M - 1)[0]
       current_hot_sum = int(c[hot_idx].sum())
       # candidate generation
       moves = candidate_moves(h, c, M, rng, cap=200)
       best_newM = M; best_sec = current_hot_sum; best_i=-1
       eval_count=0
       for i,j in moves:
          code = (min(i,j), max(i,j))
          if code in tabu: continue
          newM = eval_candidate(c,h,d_rev,i,j,n,work)
          if newM < best_newM:
              best_newM = newM; best_sec = current_hot_sum; best_i=i; best_j=j
          elif newM == best_newM:
              sec = int(work[hot_idx].sum())
              if sec < best_sec:
                  best_sec=sec; best_i=i; best_j=j
          eval_count +=1
          if eval_count%100==0 and time.time()-t_last>0.2: break
       if best_i != -1 and (best_newM < M or (best_newM == M and best_sec < current_hot_sum)):
           apply swap, c work? recompute new c using eval? maybe assign work copy after recompute.
           if M < best_M: update best
           stagnation=0
           tabu add
       else:
           # perturbation: generate random moves, accept one with newM <= M+1 maybe
           improved=False
           for _ in range(50):
              i=random zero, j=random one via while
              code...
              newM=eval...
              if newM <= M and (newM < M or throughput?) accept plateau
              elif newM == M+1 and rng.rand()<0.1: accept
              if accept: break
           if not improved:
              # do a random re-evaluate from scratch? Maybe break to outer restart
              break
           stagnation +=1
       # decay high? maybe
```

def candidate_moves(h,c,M,n, cap, rng):
    lags = np.nonzero(c >= M - 1)[0] # if too many, choose cap
    if len(lags) > 30: choose random 30 with priority? Maybe keep all if len<=30.
    moves = []
    for idx in lags:
       l = idx-(n-1)
       if l >=0:
           length = n-l
           mask = (h[:length] == 0) & (h[l:length+l] == 1)
           pos = np.nonzero(mask)[0]
       else:
           a0=-l
           length = n-a0
           mask = (h[a0:] == 0) & (h[a0+l:] == 1)
           pos = np.nonzero(mask)[0] + a0
       # For each pos, i=pos, j=pos+l
       # Optionally only pos that contribute? mask ensures.
       # To cap, if len(moves)+len(pos)>cap, sample pos
       moves.extend((int(a), int(a+l)) for a in pos)
    # Add random pairs
    random_count = min(cap-len(moves), 50)
    ...
    if len(moves)>cap: randomly sample cap
    return moves
```

def run_binary(n, seed, mean_run):
   h=[]; color=1
   remaining=n
   while remaining>0:
       length=int(rng.exponential(mean_run))+1
       if length>remaining: length=remaining
       h.extend([color]*length)
       color-=1; remaining-=length
   h=np.array(h[:n],int8)
   # adjust sum to m by flipping some entries near end/balanced
```

Implement objective (continuous) with objective value approximating max; but for returning we evaluate exact. For minimize, use:
```
def smooth_obj_tol(x, lam, n, dx):
  c = dx*np.correlate(x, 1-x, 'full')
  m = c.max()
  scaled = lam*(c - m)
  sm = scaled.max()
  return m + (sm + np.log(np.exp(scaled-sm).sum()))/lam
```
This is standard log-sum-exp centered. As lam large approximates max.
Gradient of this objective: Let u=lam(c-m). Since m=max c, u≤0, max u=0. softmax weights w = exp(u)/sum exp(u) (w sums 1). Objective F=m + (m? Wait if c centered, LSE = lam? Let's derive: L = m + (1/lam)*logsumexp(lam(c-m)) = (1/lam)logsumexp(lam c). Gradient F = (1/lam)∑_i w_i lam ∂c_i/∂x = ∑ w_i ∂c_i. Where w_i = exp(lam(c_i-m)) / ∑. Nice. So gradient = weighted average of gradients of c_i.

Gradient of c = dx*corr. For c_i (i=l) = dx ∑ d_n x_{n+l}. ∂/∂x_p = dx( d_{p-l} - x_{p+l}) (with valid). We can compute weighted average over active weights. To avoid O(n^2) per gradient, use convolution/FFT. But n=250 O(n^2) okay for limited iterations. Need implement top-level functions.

```
def smooth_gradient(x, lam, n, dx):
   d=1-x
   c=dx*np.correlate(x,d,'full')
   m=c.max(); u=lam*(c-m); u-=u.max(); w=np.exp(u); w/=w.sum()
   # compute A[p] = sum_l w_l d[p-l], B[p] = sum_l w_l x[p+l]
   # O(n^2) loops? Use np.dot for each p maybe 250*? Could vectorize matrix.
```

Simpler O(n^2) using matrix: For all p, term1 A = ∑_{r} d[r] w[p-r] valid. This is a Toeplitz matrix. Can compute by loop over p using slices:
```
A = np.zeros(n)
for p in range(n):
    # r=0..n-1, l=p-r
    # l range: -? Use slice of w_full indices n-1+p-r = (n-1+p) - r, descending from n-1+p to p.
    # equivalent w_full[p : n+p] reversed? earlier V_p term1 d_rev assigned. Here sum d[r] w_full[p+n-1-r]

Helper functions:
```
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def compute_c5(h,n):
   dx=2.0/n
   arr=np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
   c=np.correlate(arr, 1.0-arr, mode='full')
   return float(np.max(c)*dx)
```
Could be slow for large arrays but initial evaluation okay. For binary int, use int? `compute_c5` generic.

`project_feasible(h,n)`:

`make_binary_from_real(x,n,seed)`:
```
def make_binary_from_real(x,n,seed=0):
  x = np.asarray(x,float)
  if x.size != n:
    xo = np.linspace(0,2,max(x.size,1))
    xn = np.linspace(0,2,n)
    x = np.interp(xn,xo,x,0.0,0.0)
  m=n//2
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  score=x + 1e-6*rng.rand(n)
  order=np.argsort(score, kind='mergesort')[::-1]
  h=np.zeros(n,np.int8)
  h[order[:m]]=1
  return h
```
If n odd? We'll only call even; if odd, m=floor, sum not target. Could handle by continuous? We'll use even. For generic, if n%2==1, we can produce one 0.5 to sum n/2. But objective? Binary not exact. Implement:
```
if n%2==1:
   h[order[:m]]=1; h[order[m]]=0.5
```
But h dtype float? We use int8. Keep binary even.

`run_length_binary(n,seed,mean_run)`:

```
def run_length_binary(n,seed,mean_run):
   rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
   h=np.zeros(n,np.int8)
   val=1
   i=0
   while i<n:
      length=max(1,int(rng.exponential(max(1,mean_run))))
      if length>n-i: length=n-i
      h[i:i+length]=val
      i+=length; val=1-val
   # ensure sum
   m=n//2
   cur=int(h.sum())
   if cur>m:
      ones=np.flatnonzero(h==1)
      flip=min(cur-m,len(ones))
      h[ones[:flip]]=0 # maybe flip contiguous? Better random
      # use rng.choice if flip < len
   elif cur<m:
      zeros=np.flatnonzero(h==0)
      flip=m-cur
      h[zeros[rng.choice(np.size(zeros), flip, replace=False)]]=1
   return h
```
For flipping ones, choose random too.

def corr_int(h):

`evaluate_candidate(c, h, d_rev, i, j, n, work)`:
```
def evaluate_candidate(c,h,d_rev,i,j,n,work):
    work[:] = c
    # i zero
    work[i:i+n] += d_rev
    work[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h[:n-i]
    # j one
    work[j:j+n] -= d_rev
    work[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] += h[:n-j]
    work[n-1 + (j-i)] += 1
    work[n-1 + (i-j)] += 1
    return int(work.max())
```
Need if `n-i` etc int. For i=n-1, `2*n-1-i = n+1`? Wait 2n-1-(n-1)=n, slice [0:n] length n. Good. For i=n-1, `i:i+n` = [n-1:2n-1] length n. Good.
If i=0, `n-1-i = n-1`, `2n-1-i=2n-1`, slice length n.

Potential bug: term1 and term2 overlap; in-place addition of overlapping slices with `+=` using same `work` and `d_rev/h` unaffected. Good.

`apply_swap(c,h,d,i,j,n)`:
```
def apply_swap(c,h,d,i,j,n):
    newc = c.copy()
    newc[i:i+n] += d[::-1]
    newc[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h[:n-i]
    newc[j:j+n] -= d[::-1]
    newc[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] += h[:n-j]
    newc[n-1+j-i] +=1
    newc[n-1+i-j] +=1
    h[i]=1; h[j]=0; d[i]=0; d[j]=1
    return newc
```
This recomputes with old h,d; good. But after h/d update, `d[::-1]` in newc used old? We call update after modifications to newc. Fine. Need `d` passed is old; we update after.

Implement `smooth_obj` and `smooth_jac` as top-level. Gradient direct loops.

```
def smooth_obj(x, lam, n, dx):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    d = 1.0 - x
    c = dx * np.correlate(x, d, mode='full')
    m = float(c.max())
    scaled = lam * (c - m)
    # scaled max <=0; shift for stability (should be 0 but due numerical)
    scaled -= scaled.max()
    return m + float(np.log(np.exp(scaled).sum()) / lam)
```
Since scaled max becomes 0 after subtract, logsumexp includes 1; objective = m + (logsumexp(scaled_shift)+0)/lam? If scaled original max 0, after subtract max maybe 0. If due numerical not exactly 0, adding? Standard: `lse = maxs + log(sum(exp(c-maxs)))`. Here c includes lam(c-m). If we shift to exp stable: `maxs=scaled.max()`, `lse=maxs+log(sum(exp(scaled-maxs)))`; return (m+? Wait scaled=lam(c-m). LSE(scaled)/lam = (maxs + logsumexp(scaled-maxs))/lam. But F should = m + (1/lam)logsumexp(lam(c-m)). Since max scaled = 0 (approx), F = m + (maxs + logsumexp(scaled-maxs))/lam? If maxs not 0, mathematically maxs=0. Better use standard with full scaled2=lam*c:
```
scaled = lam*c
maxs=scaled.max()
return (maxs + np.log(np.exp(scaled-maxs).sum()))/lam
```
This avoids center. c values up to 1, lam up to 20000, scaled max~400k, exp after shift stable. Use this.

`smooth_jac`:
```
def smooth_jac(x, lam, n, dx):
    x=np.asarray(x,float)
    d=1-x
    c=dx*np.correlate(x,d,'full')
    scaled=lam*c
    maxs=scaled.max()
    ew=np.exp(scaled-maxs)
    w=ew/ew.sum()
    L=2*n-1
    w_full = w  # indexed lag offset n-1
    A=np.zeros(n)
    B=np.zeros(n)
    for p in range(n):
        # A[p]=dot(d, w_full[p:n+p][::-1])
        A[p] = np.dot(d, w_full[p:n+p][::-1])
        B[p] = np.dot(x, w_full[n-1-p:2*n-1-p])
    g = dx*(A-B)
    # optional subtract mean to help equality? SLSQP handles constraints; but gradient along constraint can be reduced:
    # g -= g.sum()/n
    return g
```
Check A with p=n-1: w_full[n-1:2n-1] reversed? Wait A formula `w_full[p:n+p][::-1]` gives length n. For p=n-1, slice w_full[n-1:2n-1] = indices n-1..2n-2 (lags 0..n-1), reversed lags n-1..0. Dot d[0..n-1]. But A[n-1] =∑_r d[r] w_{n-1-r}; lags n-1..0 yes correct. Good.
B p=0: w_full[n-1:2n-1] lags 0..n-1 dot x; correct.
B p=n-1: w_full[0:n] lags -(n-1)..0 dot x; correct.

But wait `w_full[p:n+p]` for p=n-1 ends 2n-1? slice excludes 2n-1, length n; index max 2n-2, good. For p=0, [0:n] lags -(n-1)..0 reversed -> 0..-(n-1); correct. B slice p=0 [n-1:L] lags 0..n-1; B[p=n-1] [0:n] lags -n+1..0. Good.

This gradient includes lags where not valid? The slices length n include all lags, and d/x length n; invalid terms? We reason valid terms correspond exactly. For p=0, A uses w lags 0,-1,...,-n+1 with d0,d1,...; correct. B uses lags 0,1,...,n-1 with x0..x n-1; correct. Good.

We can speed by precomputing reversed w? But okay.

```
def interpolate_h(x_in, n):
   m=x_in.size
   if m==n: return x_in.copy()
   dx=2.0/m; xg=np.linspace(0,2,m,endpoint=False)+dx/2
   dxn=2.0/n; xng=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)+dxn/2
   return np.interp(xng,xg,x_in)
```

Let's write `random_swap_indices` top-level:
```
def random_swap_indices(h,n,rng):
    maxtry=50
    for _ in range(maxtry):
        i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
        if i!=j and h[i]==0 and h[j]==1:
            return i,j
    # fallback
    zeros=np.nonzero(h==0)[0]; ones=np.nonzero(h==1)[0]
    if zeros.size and ones.size:
        return int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)]), int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)])
    return -1,-1
```

`sample_uniform_indices` for random moves maybe.

`threshold_top(score,n,seed)`:
```
def threshold_top(score,n,seed=0):
   score=np.asarray(score,dtype=float).reshape(-1)
   if score.size != n: score=score[:n] ...? ensure
   m=n//2
   if n%2==0:
      rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
      s=score + 1e-6*rng.random(n)
      order=np.argsort(s, kind='mergesort')[::-1]
      h=np.zeros(n,np.int8)
      h[order[:m]]=1
      return h
   else:
      top m, assign one 0.5? For binary local need even; but threshold_top could return float. For general use binary? We'll only even. If odd, set h[order[:m]]=1, h[order[m]]=0.5 dtype float? But candidate only even. Simpler if odd, return float array with 0.5.
```
But `make_binary_from_real` for even can use threshold_top. We'll ensure n even before binary.

`make_binary_from_real(x,n,seed)`:
- Ensure n even: if odd, n=n+1? But function maybe called by binary_improve with even. Do inside `ensure_even_start` before.
- Interpolate if size !=n.
- Return int8.

`random_binary(n,seed)` requires even. If not, floor.

`run_length_binary(n,seed,mean_run)` ensure even; if sum mismatch after adjust, if n even.

`sine_binary(n, seed, freq, quad, phase)`:
```
def sine_binary(n,seed,freq,quad,phase):
   x=np.arange(n,dtype=float)/n
   score=np.sin(2*np.pi*(freq*x + quad*x*x)+phase)
   return threshold_top(score,n,seed)
```
Use `threshold_top` with int8.

`candidate_moves` should accept h int8.

`binary_improve` details more precise:
```
def binary_improve(h_init, n, deadline, seed=42):
    n=int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0: n += 1
    if n < 10: n=10
    h=make_binary_from_real(h_init,n,seed)
    d=np.ones(n,dtype=np.int8)
    d -= h ? d = 1 - h => int8. In Python/numpy: d = (np.ones(n,dtype=np.int8) - h)
    d_rev=d[::-1]
    c=corr_int(h)
    M=int(c.max())
    best_M=M; best_h=h.copy()
    work=np.empty(c.shape,dtype=np.int32)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    tabu=set()
    plateau=0; last_M=M; stagnant=0
    # parameters based on n
    if n <= 300: max_cand=320; max_lags=18; per_lag=8; rnd=60
    elif n <= 500: max_cand=240; max_lags=14; per_lag=6; rnd=50
    else: max_cand=160; max_lags=10; per_lag=5; rnd=40
    while time.time() < deadline:
        if n_recent ==0? 
        if M > best_M + 3:
           h=best_h.copy(); d=...; c=corr_int(h); M=int(c.max()); tabu.clear()
        hot_idx=np.nonzero(c >= M-1)[0]
        current_sec=int(c[hot_idx].sum()) if hot_idx.size else 0
        moves=candidate_moves(h,c,M,n,rng,max_cand,max_lags,per_lag,rnd)
        best_newM=M; best_sec=current_sec; best_i=-1; best_j=-1
        evalcnt=0
        tcheck=time.time()
        for i,j in moves:
           if i<0: continue
           code=(i,j) if i<j else (j,i)
           if code in tabu: continue
           newM=evaluate_candidate(c,h,d_rev,i,j,n,work)
           evalcnt+=1
           if newM < best_newM:
               best_newM=newM; best_sec=current_sec; best_i=i; best_j=j
           elif newM == best_newM:
               sec = int(work[hot_idx].sum()) if hot_idx.size else 0
               if sec < best_sec:
                  best_sec=sec; best_i=i; best_j=j
           if (evalcnt & 255)==0:
              if time.time() - tcheck > 0.5: break
        accepted=False
        if best_i != -1 and (best_newM < M or (best_newM == M and best_sec < current_sec)):
           newc=apply_swap(c,h,d,best_i,best_j,n,d_rev) ? apply can use d_rev? We'll define apply_swap(c,h,d,i,j,n) compute d_rev inside. 
           c=newc; M=best_newM
           tabu.add((min(best_i,best_j), max(...)))
           if len(tabu)>800: tabu.clear()
           if M < best_M:
              best_M=M; best_h=h.copy(); tabu.clear(); plateau=0; last_M=M
           elif M == last_M:
              plateau += 1
              if plateau > 400:
                  # force perturbation by clearing? maybe set plateau=0 and break? 
                  pass
           else:
              plateau=0; last_M=M
           accepted=True
        if not accepted:
            # perturbation: evaluate random candidates
            pbestM=M; pbestSec=current_sec; pi=-1
            # choose best among random with newM <= M+1 maybe
            for _ in range(80):
               i,j=random_swap_indices(h,n,rng)
               if i<0: break
               code...
               if code in tabu: continue
               newM=evaluate_candidate(...)
               if newM <= M+1:
                  sec = int(work[hot_idx].sum()) if hot_idx.size else 0
                  # choose by newM, sec; for newM=M+1 require sec < pbestSec? maybe
                  if newM < pbestM or (newM==pbestM and sec<pbestSec): update
               if time.time()>deadline: break
            if pi != -1:
               newc=apply_swap(...)
               c=newc; M=pbestM
               tabu.add...
               if M < best_M: update
               else:
                  plateau += 1
               if plateau > 500: break
            else:
               stagnant += 1
               if stagnant > 8:
                  # perturb harsher: take best_h plus random swaps? But maybe just break
                  break
               # reset current to best_h? Maybe do random swaps from best
               if M >= best_M + 1 or stagnant%2==0:
                   h=best_h.copy(); d=...; c=corr_int(h); M=int(c.max())

        if time.time()>deadline: break
    best_hf=best_h.astype(float)
    cval=best_M*2.0/float(n)
    # optional exact compute
    # cval=compute_c5(best_hf,n)
    return best_hf, cval, n
```
Need `apply_swap` signature: It can compute `d_rev = d[::-1]` and use full h. But if we pass `d` and `h` mutable, after creating newc, update h,d.
```
def apply_swap(c,h,d,i,j,n):
    d_rev=d[::-1]
    newc=c.copy()
    newc[i:i+n] += d_rev
    newc[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h
    newc[j:j+n] -= d_rev
    newc[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] += h
    newc[n-1] -= 2
    newc[n-1+j-i] += 1
    newc[n-1+i-j] += 1
    if (h[i] != 0 or h[j] != 1): # maybe called when not binary valid? But should.
       # If not, cannot just flip? We'll assume.
    h[i]=1; h[j]=0; d[i]=0; d[j]=1
    return newc
```
Note: term2 `newc[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h` uses full h length n. Slices length exactly n. For i=0: end 2n-1, ok. For i=n-1: start0,end n, ok.
`d` assigned int8. `newc` int32; subtract int8 full okay.

`evaluate_candidate` similar but not mutate h/d:
```
def evaluate_candidate(c,h,d_rev,i,j,n,work):
   work[:]=c
   work[i:i+n] += d_rev
   work[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h
   work[j:j+n] -= d_rev
   work[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] += h
   work[n-1] -= 2
   work[n-1+j-i] += 1
   work[n-1+i-j] += 1
   return int(work.max())
```
If `work` is int32, okay. `work[n-1+j-i]` index within 0..2n-2 because j-i ∈[-n+1,n-1]. Good.

Potential issue in `apply_swap`: If i,j not as expected (h[i]!=0/h[j]!=1) due to bug/random after c update? Should ensure before. In binary_improve, random_swap_indices checks. Candidate generated from h. Good.

`corr_int` for h int8 returns int32; if h sum not n/2? local search preserves sum. If initial make_binary ensures.

`make_binary_from_real`: If `n` even but x size maybe odd; interpolate. Ensure sum m.
```
def make_binary_from_real(x, n, seed=0):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float)
    if x.ndim>1: x=x.reshape(-1)
    if x.size == 0 or n%2!=0: n = n + (1 if n%2 else 0)
    if x.size != n:
       x=interpolate_h(x,n)
    return threshold_top(x,n,seed)
```
But `threshold_top` returns int8 for even.

`threshold_top` if n even:

`interpolate_h` for x size 1? If size 0 handle.
```
def interpolate_h(x,n):
   x=np.asarray(x,float).reshape(-1)
   if x.size==0: return np.full(n,0.5)
   if x.size==n: return x.copy()
   m=x.size
   dxo=2.0/m; xo=np.linspace(0,2,m,endpoint=False)+dxo/2
   dxn=2.0/n; xn=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)+dxn/2
   return np.interp(xn,xo,x,left=x[0],right=x[-1])
```

`continuous_refine(h,n,lam_list,deadline)`:
```
def continuous_refine(h,n,lam_list,deadline):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best=compute_c5(h,n); best_h=h.copy()
    for lam in lam_list:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       maxiter=int(max(30, min(250, rem/6)))
       h2=slsqp_smooth(h,n,lam,maxiter)
       if h2 is None: h2=h
       try: c2=compute_c5(h2,n)
       except: c2=best
       if c2 < best - 1e-12:
           best=c2; best_h=h2.copy(); h=h2.copy()
       else:
           # still continue maybe
           pass
    return best_h,best
```
SLSQP may take poorly; maxiter based on rem/6, if rem large 1000 =>250. Good.

def sum_constraint(x,n): return float(np.sum(x)-0.5*float(n))

Implement:
```
def evaluate_fast(h,n):
    arr=np.asarray(h)
    if arr.dtype == np.int8:
        c=corr_int(arr)
        return float(c.max())*2.0/float(n)
    if arr.dtype == np.int32:
        c=np.correlate(arr.astype(np.int32), 1-arr.astype(np.int32), 'full')
        return float(c.max())*2.0/float(n)
    return compute_c5(h,n)
```
No int8 in isinstance? Use `arr.dtype.kind in 'iu'`? But continuous 0/1 int? If int, use corr_int after astype int8 maybe values 0/1. If integer but not 0/1? shouldn't.
```
if arr.dtype.kind in 'iu':
   h8=arr.astype(np.int8, copy=False)
   c=corr_int(h8)
   return float(c.max())*2.0/n
```

`project_feasible` binary search: If x sum near target, return before projection. If not, lo/hi. Suppose x all 0, target n/2. lo=-1, hi=1. x-mid clip. For mid=-0.5, x-mid=0.5 sum n > target, lo=-0.5. For mid=0, sum 0 < target, hi=0. Eventually mid negative, x-mid positive small? Need target half, tau negative. Sum decreases with tau; at tau=-0.5 sum n; tau=0 sum0. We need tau = -0.5? For x all0, clip(0 - tau)=clip(-tau) = -tau if tau<0. Sum = -tau n. target n/2 => tau=-0.5. Binary search condition: if s > target, sum too high, tau too low? At tau=-0.6 sum0.6n>0.5, need increase tau toward 0, set lo=mid? lo initially -1, hi1. mid0: s=0<target => hi=0. mid -0.5 s=0.5 equal. Works. If s>target, smaller tau gives larger x, so need bigger tau: lo=mid (since tau in [lo,hi], lo is lower). Good.

After project, `x = clip(x - tau)`. If x all0, tau=-0.5 -> x=0.5 sum exact. Good.

`candidate_moves` code:
```
def candidate_moves(h,c,M,n,rng,max_cand,max_lags,per_lag,random_pairs):
    moves=[]; used=set()
    idx=np.nonzero(c >= max(0,M-1))[0]
    if idx.size > max_lags:
        order=np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]
        half=max(1,max_lags//2)
        top=order[:half]
        rest=order[half:]
        if rest.size:
           take=min(rest.size, max_lags-half)
           samp=rest[rng.choice(take, take? Wait need sample without replacement:
              samp = rest[rng.choice(rest.size, take, replace=False)]
           idx = idx[np.concatenate((top, samp))]
        else:
           idx = idx[order[:max_lags]]
    else:
        if idx.size > max_lags: idx=idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1][:max_lags]]
    for li in idx:
       l=int(li-(n-1))
       a_start = 0 if l>=0 else -l
       a_end = n-l if l>=0 else n  # since n-l = n+|l| >n; use min
       if l >=0:
          a_start=0; a_end=n-l
       else:
          a_start=-l; a_end=n
       length=a_end-a_start
       if length<=0: continue
       mask = (h[a_start:a_end] == 0) & (h[a_start+l:a_end+l] == 1)
       pos=np.nonzero(mask)[0] + a_start
       if pos.size ==0: continue
       if pos.size > per_lag:
           chosen=pos[rng.choice(pos.size, per_lag, replace=False)]
       else: chosen=pos
       for p in chosen:
          i=int(p); j=int(p+l)
          if i<0 or j<0: continue
          code = i*n+j if i<j else j*n+i
          if code not in used:
             used.add(code); moves.append((i,j))
       if len(moves) >= max_cand-random_pairs: break
    rnd = max(0, max_cand-len(moves))
    for _ in range(rnd):
       i,j=random_swap_indices(h,n,rng)
       if i<0: break
       code = i*n+j if i<j else j*n+i
       if code not in used:
          used.add(code); moves.append((i,j))
    return moves
```
If `random_pairs` > max_cand-len(moves), okay. But random_swap_indices function callsrng; it may be in candidate_moves, okay. Could be overhead but fine.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    c = np.correlate(arr, 1.0 - arr, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c)) * dx


def evaluate_fast(h, n):
    arr = np.asarray(h)
    if arr.dtype.kind in "iu":
        c = corr_int(arr.astype(np.int8, copy=False))
        return float(np.max(c)) * (2.0 / float(n))
    return compute_c5(h, n)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    x = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    if abs(float(np.sum(x)) - target) <= 1e-12:
        return x

    lo = float(np.min(x)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(x)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.sum(np.clip(x - mid, 0.0, 1.0)))
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid

    tau = 0.5 * (lo + Hi if False else (lo + hi))
    x = np.clip(x - tau, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        free = np.where((x > 1e-12) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-12))[0]
        if free.size:
            x[free] += diff / float(free.size)
            x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(np.sum(x))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        slack = np.minimum(x, 1.0 - x)
        i = int(np.argmax(slack))
        val = target - (float(np.sum(x)) - float(x[i]))
        if val >= 0.0 and val <= 1.0:
            x[i] = val
    return x


def interpolate_h(x, n):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.full(int(n), 0.5)
    if x.size == int(n):
        return x.copy()
    m = int(x.size)
    dxo = 2.0 / float(m)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, m, endpoint=False) + 0.5 * dxo
    dxn = 2.0 / float(n)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(n), endpoint=False) + 0.5 * dxn
    return np.interp(xn, xo, x, left=float(x[0]), right=float(x[-1]))


def threshold_top(score, n, seed=0):
    score = np.asarray(score, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if score.size < int(n):
        score = np.resize(score, int(n))
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        return project_feasible(score[:int(n)] if int(n) >= n else score, n)
    m = n // 2
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    s = score.astype(float, copy=True)
    s += 1e-9 * rng.random_sample(n)
    order = np.argsort(s, kind="mergesort")[::-1]
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    h[order[:m]] = 1
    return h


def make_binary_from_real(x, n, seed=0):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n += 1
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
    if x.size != n:
        x = interpolate_h(x, n)
    return threshold_top(x, n, seed)


def random_binary(n, seed=0):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n += 1
    m = n // 2
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    if m:
        h[rng.choice(n, m, replace=False)] = 1
    return h


def run_length_binary(n, seed=0, mean_run=8.0):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n += 1
    m = n // 2
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int8)
    val = 1
    i = 0
    while i < n:
        length = max(1, int(rng.exponential(max(1.0, float(mean_run)))))
        if length > n - i:
            length = n - i
        h[i:i + length] = val
        i += length
        val = 1 - val

    cur = int(np.sum(h))
    if cur > m:
        ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1)
        flip = min(cur - m, int(ones.size))
        if flip > 0:
            if flip < ones.size:
                sel = rng.choice(ones.size, flip, replace=False)
                h[ones[sel]] = 0
            else:
                h[ones] = 0
    elif cur < m:
        zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
        flip = m - cur
        if flip > 0 and zeros.size:
            if flip < zeros.size:
                sel = rng.choice(zeros.size, flip, replace=False)
                h[zeros[sel]] = 1
            else:
                h[zeros] = 1
    return h


def sine_binary(n, seed=0, freq=4.0, quad=0.0, phase=0.0):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n += 1
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=float) / float(n)
    score = np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (freq * t + quad * t * t) + phase)
    return threshold_top(score, n, seed)


def corr_int(h):
    hi = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.int32)
    di = 1 - hi
    return np.correlate(hi, di, mode="full").astype(np.int32, copy=False)


def random_swap_indices(h, n, rng):
    for _ in range(80):
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i != j and int(h[i]) == 0 and int(h[j]) == 1:
            return i, j
    zeros = np.flatnonzero(h == 0)
    ones = np.flatnonzero(h == 1)
    if zeros.size and ones.size:
        return int(zeros[rng.randint(zeros.size)]), int(ones[rng.randint(ones.size)])
    return -1, -1


def candidate_moves(h, c, M, n, rng, max_cand, max_lags, per_lag, random_pairs):
    moves = []
    used = set()

    if M > 0:
        idx = np.nonzero(c >= max(0, M - 1))[0]
    else:
        idx = np.nonzero(c >= 0)[0]

    if idx.size > max_lags:
        order = np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1]
        half = max(1, max_lags // 2)
        top = order[:half]
        rest = order[half:]
        if rest.size:
            take = min(int(rest.size), max_lags - half)
            sample = rest[rng.choice(rest.size, take, replace=False)]
            selected = np.concatenate((top, sample))
        else:
            selected = order[:max_lags]
        idx = idx[selected]
    else:
        if idx.size > max_lags:
            idx = idx[np.argsort(c[idx])[::-1][:max_lags]]

    for li in idx:
        l = int(li - (n - 1))
        if l >= 0:
            a_start = 0
            a_end = n - l
        else:
            a_start = -l
            a_end = n
        length = a_end - a_start
        if length <= 0:
            continue
        mask = (h[a_start:a_end] == 0) & (h[a_start + l:a_end + l] == 1)
        pos = np.nonzero(mask)[0] + a_start
        if pos.size == 0:
            continue
        if pos.size > per_lag:
            chosen = pos[rng.choice(pos.size, per_lag, replace=False)]
        else:
            chosen = pos
        for p in chosen:
            i = int(p)
            j = int(p + l)
            if i < 0 or j < 0 or i >= n or j >= n:
                continue
            if int(h[i]) != 0 or int(h[j]) != 1:
                continue
            code = i * n + j if i < j else j * n + i
            if code not in used:
                used.add(code)
                moves.append((i, j))
        if len(moves) >= max_cand - random_pairs:
            break

    for _ in range(max(0, max_cand - len(moves))):
        i, j = random_swap_indices(h, n, rng)
        if i < 0:
            break
        code = i * n + j if i < j else j * n + i
        if code not in used:
            used.add(code)
            moves.append((i, j))
    return moves


def evaluate_candidate(c, h, d_rev, i, j, n, work):
    work[:] = c

    work[i:i + n] += d_rev
    work[n - 1 - i:2 * n - 1 - i] -= h

    work[j:j + n] -= d_rev
    work[n - 1 - j:2 * n - 1 - j] += h

    work[n - 1] -= 2
    work[n - 1 + (j - i)] += 1
    work[n - 1 + (i - j)] += 1

    return int(np.max(work))


def apply_swap(c, h, d, i, j, n):
    d_rev = d[::-1]
    newc = c.copy()

    newc[i:i + n] += d_rev
    newc[n - 1 - i:2 * n - 1 - i] -= h

    newc[j:j + n] -= d_rev
    newc[n - 1 - j:2 * n - 1 - j] += h

    newc[n - 1] -= 2
    newc[n - 1 + (j - i)] += 1
    newc[n - 1 + (i - j)] += 1

    h[i] = 1
    h[j] = 0
    d[i] = 0
    d[j] = 1
    return newc


def binary_improve(h_init, n, deadline, seed=42):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n += 1
    if n < 12:
        n = 12

    h = make_binary_from_real(h_init, n, seed)
    d = (np.ones(n, dtype=np.int8) - h).astype(np.int8, copy=False)
    d_rev = d[::-1]
    c = corr_int(h)
    M = int(np.max(c))

    best_M = M
    best_h = h.copy()
    work = np.empty(c.shape, dtype=np.int32)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    tabu = set()
    last_M = M
    plateau = 0
    stagnant = 0

    if n <= 300:
        max_cand = 320
        max_lags = 18
        per_lag = 8
        random_pairs = 60
    elif n <= 500:
        max_cand = 240
        max_lags = 14
        per_lag = 6
        random_pairs = 50
    else:
        max_cand = 160
        max_lags = 10
        per_lag = 5
        random_pairs = 40

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if M > best_M + 3:
            h = best_h.copy()
            d = (np.ones(n, dtype=np.int8) - h).astype(np.int8, copy=False)
            d_rev = d[::-1]
            c = corr_int(h)
            M = int(np.max(c))
            tabu.clear()
            last_M = M
            plateau = 0

        hot_idx = np.nonzero(c >= M - 1)[0]
        current_sec = int(np.sum(c[hot_idx])) if hot_idx.size else 0

        moves = candidate_moves(h, c, M, n, rng, max_cand, max_lags, per_lag, random_pairs)

        best_new_M = M
        best_sec = current_sec
        best_i = -1
        best_j = -1

        t_check = time.time()
        cnt = 0
        for i, j in moves:
            code = i * n + j if i < j else j * n + i
            if code in tabu:
                continue
            new_M = evaluate_candidate(c, h, d_rev, i, j, n, work)
            cnt += 1

            if new_M < best_new_M:
                best_new_M = new_M
                best_sec = current_sec
                best_i = i
                best_j = j
            elif new_M == best_new_M:
                sec = int(np.sum(work[hot_idx])) if hot_idx.size else 0
                if sec < best_sec:
                    best_sec = sec
                    best_i = i
                    best_j = j

            if (cnt & 255) == 0 and time.time() - t_check > 0.5:
                break

        accepted = False
        if best_i != -1 and (best_new_M < M or (best_new_M == M and best_sec < current_sec)):
            c = apply_swap(c, h, d, best_i, best_j, n)
            M = best_new_M

            code = best_i * n + best_j if best_i < best_j else best_j * n + best_i
            tabu.add(code)
            if len(tabu) > 1200:
                tabu.clear()

            if M < best_M:
                best_M = M
                best_h = h.copy()
                tabu.clear()
                last_M = M
                plateau = 0
            elif M == last_M:
                plateau += 1
            else:
                last_M = M
                plateau = 0
            accepted = True

        if not accepted:
            pbest_M = M + 2
            pbest_sec = current_sec
            pi = -1
            pj = -1

            for _ in range(80):
                i, j = random_swap_indices(h, n, rng)
                if i < 0:
                    break
                code = i * n + j if i < j else j * n + i
                if code in tabu:
                    continue
                new_M = evaluate_candidate(c, h, d_rev, i, j, n, work)
                if new_M <= M + 1:
                    sec = int(np.sum(work[hot_idx])) if hot_idx.size else 0
                    if new_M < pbest_M or (new_M == pbest_M and sec < pbest_sec):
                        pbest_M = new_M
                        pbest_sec = sec
                        pi = i
                        pj = j
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break

            if pi != -1:
                c = apply_swap(c, h, d, pi, pj, n)
                M = pbest_M

                code = pi * n + pj if pi < pj else pj * n + pi
                tabu.add(code)
                if len(tabu) > 1200:
                    tabu.clear()

                if M < best_M:
                    best_M = M
                    best_h = h.copy()
                    tabu.clear()
                    last_M = M
                    plateau = 0
                elif M == last_M:
                    plateau += 1
                else:
                    last_M = M
                    plateau = 0

                if plateau > 600:
                    break
            else:
                stagnant += 1
                if stagnant > 6:
                    break

                if M >= best_M + 1 or stagnant % 2 == 0:
                    h = best_h.copy()
                    d = (np.ones(n, dtype=np.int8) - h).astype(np.int8, copy=False)
                    d_rev = d[::-1]
                    c = corr_int(h)
                    M = int(np.max(c))
                    for _ in range(int(rng.randint(3, 8))):
                        i, j = random_swap_indices(h, n, rng)
                        if i >= 0:
                            c = apply_swap(c, h, d, i, j, n)
                    M = int(np.max(c))
                    tabu.clear()
                    stagnant = 0
                    last_M = M
                    plateau = 0

        if plateau > 900:
            break

    best_h = best_h.astype(float)
    c5 = float(best_M) * (2.0 / float(n))
    return best_h, c5, int(n)


def sum_constraint(x, n):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 0.5 * float(n))


def sum_jacobian(x, n):
    return np.ones(int(n), dtype=float)


def smooth_obj(x, lam, n, dx):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    d = 1.0 - x
    c = dx * np.correlate(x, d, mode="full")
    scaled = lam * c
    maxs = float(np.max(scaled))
    ew = np.exp(scaled - maxs)
    return float((maxs + np.log(float(np.sum(ew)))) / lam)


def smooth_jac(x, lam, n, dx):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    d = 1.0 - x
    c = dx * np.correlate(x, d, mode="full")
    scaled = lam * c
    maxs = float(np.max(scaled))
    ew = np.exp(scaled - maxs)
    w = ew / float(np.sum(ew))

    A = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    B = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for p in range(n):
        A[p] = float(np.dot(d, w[p:n + p][::-1]))
        B[p] = float(np.dot(x, w[n - 1 - p:2 * n - 1 - p]))

    g = dx * (A - B)
    g -= float(np.sum(g)) / float(n)
    return g


def slsqp_smooth(h, n, lam, maxiter):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    h0 = project_feasible(h, n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(n)
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": sum_constraint, "jac": sum_jacobian, "args": (int(n),)}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_obj,
            h0,
            args=(float(lam), int(n), float(dx)),
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            jac=smooth_jac,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-12, "maxls": 20},
        )
        return project_feasible(res.x, int(n))
    except Exception:
        return h0


def continuous_refine(h, n, lam_list, deadline):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(h, n)

    for lam in lam_list:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        rem = max(5.0, float(deadline - time.time()))
        maxiter = int(max(20, min(60, rem / 4.0)))
        h2 = slsqp_smooth(h, n, int(lam), maxiter)
        try:
            c2 = compute_c5(h2, n)
        except Exception:
            c2 = best_c
        if c2 < best_c - 1e-12:
            best_c = c2
            best_h = h2.copy()
            h = h2.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = max(10.0, float(budget_s))
    margin = min(5.0, max(0.5, 0.005 * budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - margin)

    try:
        p = initial_h_values
        if p is None:
            parent = None
            n0 = 250
        else:
            parent = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).reshape(-1)
            if parent.size == 0:
                parent = None
                n0 = 250
            else:
                n0 = int(parent.size)
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        n0 = 250

    if parent is not None:
        best_n = n0
        best_h = project_feasible(parent, best_n)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    else:
        best_n = 250
        best_h = np.full(best_n, 0.5)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    quick_time = min(25.0, max(5.0, 0.1 * budget_s))
    quick_end = min(deadline, t0 + quick_time)

    base_ns = []
    if parent is not None:
        for delta in range(0, 130):
            nn = n0 + delta
            if nn % 2 == 0 and 150 <= nn <= 650:
                base_ns.append(nn)
    for nn in [240, 250, 258, 260, 264, 270, 280, 300, 320, 350, 370, 400, 450, 500]:
        base_ns.append(nn)

    seen_ns = []
    for nn in base_ns:
        nn = int(nn)
        if nn % 2 == 0 and 120 <= nn <= 700 and nn not in seen_ns:
            seen_ns.append(nn)

    candidates = []
    candidates.append((best_c, best_n, best_h.copy()))

    for nn in seen_ns[:12]:
        if time.time() > quick_end:
            break
        if parent is not None:
            hc = project_feasible(interpolate_h(parent, nn), nn)
        else:
            hc = np.full(nn, 0.5)
        cc = evaluate_fast(hc, nn)
        candidates.append((cc, nn, hc))
        if cc < best_c:
            best_c = cc
            best_n = nn
            best_h = hc.copy()

    bin_ns = []
    for nn in seen_ns[:6]:
        if nn not in bin_ns:
            bin_ns.append(nn)
    for nn in [250, 258, 260, 300, 350]:
        if nn % 2 == 0 and nn not in bin_ns:
            bin_ns.append(nn)

    for nn in bin_ns:
        if time.time() > quick_end:
            break
        for s in [int(seed), int(seed) + 17, int(seed) + 91]:
            hb = random_binary(nn, s)
            cb = evaluate_fast(hb, nn)
            candidates.append((cb, nn, hb))
            if cb < best_c:
                best_c = cb
                best_n = nn
                best_h = hb.astype(float)

        for freq, quad, phase in [(3.0, 0.0, 0.0),
                                  (4.0, 0.5, 0.3),
                                  (5.0, 1.0, 0.0),
                                  (6.0, 0.0, 0.7),
                                  (8.0, 0.3, 0.5)]:
            if time.time() > quick_end:
                break
            hb = sine_binary(nn, int(seed) + 101 + int(freq), freq, quad, phase)
            cb = evaluate_fast(hb, nn)
            candidates.append((cb, nn, hb))
            if cb < best_c:
                best_c = cb
                best_n = nn
                best_h = hb.astype(float)

        for mean_run in [6.0, 10.0, 15.0]:
            if time.time() > quick_end:
                break
            hb = run_length_binary(nn, int(seed) + 203 + int(10 * mean_run), mean_run)
            cb = evaluate_fast(hb, nn)
            candidates.append((cb, nn, hb))
            if cb < best_c:
                best_c = cb
                best_n = nn
                best_h = hb.astype(float)

    if len(candidates) > 1:
        cvals = np.zeros(len(candidates), dtype=float)
        for i in range(len(candidates)):
            cvals[i] = candidates[i][0]
        order = np.argsort(cvals)
        ordered = []
        for k in order:
            ordered.append(candidates[int(k)])
        candidates = ordered

    starts = []
    seen_start_n = set()
    for item in candidates:
        if len(starts) >= 7:
            break
        _, nn, hh = item
        nn = int(nn)
        if nn % 2 != 0:
            continue
        if nn in seen_start_n:
            continue
        seen_start_n.add(nn)
        starts.append((nn, hh.copy()))

    for nn in [250, 258, 260, 300, 350]:
        if len(starts) >= 7:
            break
        if nn in seen_start_n:
            continue
        if parent is not None:
            hh = project_feasible(interpolate_h(parent, nn), nn)
        else:
            hh = random_binary(nn, int(seed) + 555)
        seen_start_n.add(nn)
        starts.append((nn, hh))

    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline - time.time()))
    if rem > 40.0 and starts:
        t_bin1 = time.time() + 0.55 * rem
        per = max(0.0, (t_bin1 - time.time()) / float(len(starts)))
        for idx in range(len(starts)):
            sub_deadline = min(t_bin1, time.time() + per)
            if sub_deadline - time.time() < 15.0:
                break
            nn, hh = starts[idx]
            try:
                hb, cb, nb = binary_improve(hh, nn, sub_deadline, int(seed) + 111 + idx)
                if cb < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = cb
                    best_n = int(nb)
                    best_h = hb.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass
            if time.time() > t_bin1 - 5.0:
                break

    cont_starts = []
    if parent is not None:
        cont_starts.append((n0, project_feasible(parent, n0)))
    if best_h.size:
        cont_starts.append((int(best_n), best_h.copy()))
    sm = project_feasible(np.clip(0.15 + 0.70 * best_h, 0.0, 1.0), int(best_n))
    cont_starts.append((int(best_n), sm))

    dedup = []
    seen_cont_n = set()
    for nn, hh in cont_starts:
        nn = int(nn)
        if nn in seen_cont_n or nn < 50 or nn > 700:
            continue
        seen_cont_n.add(nn)
        dedup.append((nn, hh))
        if len(dedup) >= 3:
            break

    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline - time.time()))
    if rem > 120.0 and dedup:
        t_cont = time.time() + min(rem * 0.25, 180.0)
        per = max(0.0, (t_cont - time.time()) / float(len(dedup)))
        for idx in range(len(dedup)):
            sub_deadline = min(t_cont, time.time() + per)
            if sub_deadline - time.time() < 25.0:
                break
            nn, hh = dedup[idx]
            lam_list = [200, 1000, 4000] if sub_deadline - time.time() > 90.0 else [500, 2000]
            try:
                hb, cb = continuous_refine(hh, nn, lam_list, sub_deadline)
                if cb < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = cb
                    best_n = int(nn)
                    best_h = hb.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass
            if time.time() > t_cont - 5.0:
                break

    rem = max(0.0, float(deadline - time.time()))
    if rem > 40.0:
        hstart = best_h.copy()
        nstart = int(best_n)
        if nstart % 2:
            nstart += 1
            hstart = interpolate_h(hstart, nstart)
        try:
            hb, cb, nb = binary_improve(hstart, nstart, deadline - 2.0, int(seed) + 1234)
            if cb < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c = cb
                best_n = int(nb)
                best_h = hb.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if deadline - time.time() > 45.0 and parent is not None:
        for nn in [258, 260, 270, 300]:
            if deadline - time.time() < 30.0:
                break
            hh = project_feasible(interpolate_h(parent, nn), nn)
            try:
                hb, cb, nb = binary_improve(hh, nn, deadline - 2.0, int(seed) + 2345 + nn)
                if cb < best_c - 1e-12:
                    best_c = cb
                    best_n = int(nb)
                    best_h = hb.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, int(best_n))
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, int(best_n))
    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
